# Card effects: documented functions (`duel_effects`)

Reference table generated in the documentation round of 2026-10-31. Each row is a function that reached the `documented` stage (`docs/PROGRESS.md`): its prototype, purpose, return value, side effects and callers, read from the Ghidra decompilation with the names of that round. The same text is the function's plate comment in Ghidra (`re/documented*.tsv`, applied by `tools/ghidra/ApplySignatures.java`). How the subsystem works as a whole is in [`../duel_effects.md`](../duel_effects.md); pointers to structures are typed `void *` and the structure is named in the summary.

Functions: 1269 (rel_duel_eng 1269).

| `0x09bf541c` | `uint duel_effects_get_link_damaged_sides(void *link)` | Calls duel_effects_get_link_lp_change_signs(link, signs[2]) and, for r = 0 (owner) and 1 (opponent) with side = owner xor r, sets bit r of the result when side can take effect damage (duel_core_can_take_effect_damage(side, 0)) and the change is negative without duel_core_is_damage_converted_to_gain, or positive with duel_core_is_lp_gain_reversed: the relative sides that would lose Life Points.
